Feminism is a range of social movements, political movements, and ideologies that aim to define, establish, and achieve the political, economic, personal, and social equality of the sexes. Feminism incorporates the position that societies prioritize the male point of view, and that women are treated unjustly within those societies. Efforts to change this include fighting gender stereotypes and seeking to establish educational and professional opportunities for women that are equal to those for men.\n\nKey aspects of feminism include:\n\n1. **Equality**: Feminists advocate for women to have the same legal, social, and political rights as men. This includes equal opportunities in education, employment, and political participation.\n\n2. **Intersectionality**: Many feminists recognize that women's experiences are not homogeneous and can be influenced by other factors such as race, class, sexual orientation, and disability. Intersectional feminism aims to address these overlapping forms of discrimination.\n\n3. **Critique of Patriarchy**: Feminism often involves a critique of patriarchal systems and norms, which are seen as perpetuating gender inequality.\n\n4. **Activism**: Feminism often involves activism and advocacy aimed at bringing about social change. This can take many forms, from grassroots organizing to policy advocacy.\n\n5. **Diversity**: There are many types of feminism, including liberal feminism, radical feminism, cultural feminism, eco-feminism, and postmodern feminism, each with its own perspectives and priorities.\n\nFeminism has historically been divided into several waves, with the first wave focusing on suffrage and property rights, the second wave on cultural and social inequalities, and the third wave on individuality and diversity. Chosen 优于 Rejected，主要依据是 Chosen 提供了更全面的细节（如具体的流派名称、历史分波、交叉性概念等），而 Rejected 回答较为简略且存在细微表述瑕疵（如将流派称为'branches'而非'types'或'waves'）。\n\n根本原因在于评分标准的设计缺陷：现有的标准（特别是 Criterion 8/12/17 '事实准确性'）仅设置了二元通过门槛（'是否包含虚假信息'），未能有效区分'信息丰富度'和'解释深度'。Rejected 回答虽然满足了'无虚假'的低级要求，但缺乏 Chosen 回答所具备的实质性细节（如具体流派列举、历史演变描述），导致 Judge 无法根据现有标准识别出两者在'质量'上的显著差距。这符合经验 ID 6 的触发场景：当任务要求生成内容概述或解释时，评分标准不能仅将'提及'或'涵盖'作为唯一正向条件，必须显式区分'概念覆盖'与'实质阐述'。当前的 Rubric 未强制要求模型展示具体的支撑细节（如具体的理论名称、数据结果、因果逻辑），使得简略但正确的回答与详尽准确的回答在评分上无法拉开差距。\n\n因此，需要引入一条新的评分标准设计经验，指导 Generator 在面对此类解释性任务时，将抽象的'提及'拆解为包含具体验证点的子项，以确保评分能识别出'有骨架无血肉'的回答。", "candidates": [{"method": {"trigger": "当任务要求基于特定主题生成内容概述、解释或定义，且该主题涉及具体论点、数据、方法或理论框架的展开时", "principle": "评分标准不能仅将'提及'或'涵盖'某一概念作为唯一正向条件，必须显式区分'概念覆盖'与'实质阐述'；若回答仅罗列关键词而未提供具体的支撑细节（如具体理论名称、数据结果、因果逻辑），即使命中所有关键词，也不应视为完全满足要求。", "application_guide": "Generator
